## Turn 6
> examine the book

You turn to the book. The first page is badly printed. The second is worse.

By the third, you are somewhere else.

Torchlight catches on damp stone as the tunnel opens into a shallow alcove. A low table of unfinished rock sits against the left wall. Six sealed caskets rest on it in a neat row, each lid bound with iron straps and a simple latch. Five lids are unmarked. The sixth bears your name, cut cleanly into the wood as if the carver knew you would come. The air here is still; farther ahead the tunnel continues into deeper dark.

You turn to the book. The page is still under your thumb.

The sixth bears your name, cut cleanly into the wood as if the carver knew you would come. The air here is still; farther ahead the tunnel continues into deeper dark.
